## Stage 2 rehearsal recovery — Git path parsing corrected
- First read-only rehearsal [43523267849](https://github.com/zonetrooper90/StrawVerse-mac/actions/runs/43523267849) stopped in preparation before baseline mutation, at request path validation. Both independent checks on `93d3c58510b246f92916f91bdac30b7ff0b9e413` passed; inspected actual branch and unchanged accepted Q2-B1 checkpoint SHA-256 `2c0dbfd0aa3ff31fd36d0f0b82ae3421351a128cd6e9c39f46fbde232d24d608`. No candidate uploaded, no browser jobs started, no application/checkpoint changes.
- Root cause reproduced in a disposable local Git repo: `names()` appended `-z` after `--` and the pathspec, so Git treated it as an additional pathspec and returned a newline-terminated filename. Move `-z` immediately after the Git subcommand and assert nonempty results end with NUL. Add real temporary-Git regression `gallery/tests/test_publisher_candidate_paths.py` covering exact request path, filtered empty results, staged names and paths with spaces; mandatory before prepare. Rehearsal trigger includes its shared workflow/helper/test paths so fixes rerun the identical read-only rehearsal. All existing checks and publication restrictions remain intact. Corrected full rehearsal and both independent checks passed on exact infrastructure commit `f9748172d1a3ce9dd07b740762a34485c6c3badf`; see verified results below.

- Static source inspection of accepted checkpoint found narrow-width feasibility blocker: at <=460px, card flex-basis is 76% with max-width 245px (02-anime-detail.css); at <=560px both active edge overlays are 54px each (07-anime-detail-refinements.css). At 370px viewport, workspace horizontal padding 17px each leaves at most 336px rail width; the central unobstructed interval is at most 228px, while a 336px rail has a 245px card. More generally, for a rail width R<=336px, min(0.76R,245)>R-108, so no interior scroll position fully reveals a middle card when both arrows are active. Static CSS geometry, not measured browser output. Need real-DOM validation of widths and proximity snap behavior. Proposed only: adapt narrow overlay width to available card clearance (e.g. cap at 12% of rail width below 460px), retain arrow icon, gradient, full-height hit target and desktop widths; verify actual dimensions and keyboard/pointer/guard accessibility before delivery. User approval of layout adjustment required before implementing; alternative narrower cards or moved arrows are not approved. No app or checkpoint changes. The already approved behavior remains pending.

### Stage 4 exact-commit audit diagnostic and scoped test-only correction (2026-11-22)
